<script type="text/javascript">
    $(document).ready(function () {
        // Tooltip
        $('[data-toggle="tooltip"]').tooltip();
        $('[data-toggle="popover"]').popover();
        $('body').on('click', '*', function () {
            $(this).tooltip('hide')
        });
        // Tooltip


        //
        $('a[data-toggle="tab"]').on('show.bs.tab', function (e) {
            localStorage.setItem('activeTab', $(e.target).attr('href'));
        });
        var activeTab = localStorage.getItem('activeTab');
        if (activeTab) {
            $('#sportsTab a[href="' + activeTab + '"]').tab('show');
        }
        // After tab change clear under over selection
        // or even this one if we want the earlier event
        $("a[href='#tab_target_id']").on('show.bs.tab', function (e) {
            console.log('show - before the new tab has been shown');
        });
    });

    $(document).ready
    {

        $('.btn-addToBlog').tooltip();
        $('.spTip').tooltip();
        $('.timeTip').tooltip();
        $('.btsPredTip').tooltip();
        $('.uo25PredTip').tooltip();


        function changeStyle(className, style) {
            x = document.getElementsByClassName(className);
            var i;
            for (i = 0; i < x.length; i++) {
                x[i].style.display = style;
            }

        }

        function changeWinLose(style) {
            document.getElementById("Heading_Blog_Home_Win_Soccer").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_Blog_Draw_Soccer").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_Home_Last5").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_Away_Last5").style.display = style;
            //document.getElementById("Heading_Prediction_Soccer").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_Blog_Away_Win_Soccer").style.display = style;
            //document.getElementById("Heading_Result_Prediction_Soccer").style.display = style;
            changeStyle('winLose_Soccer', style);
        }

        function changeOverUnder(style) {
            document.getElementById("Heading_UnderOverPrediction").style.display = style;
            //document.getElementById("Heading_Over").style.display = style;
            //document.getElementById("Heading_Under").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_Home_Last5_OU").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_Away_Last5_OU").style.display = style;
            changeStyle('underOver', style);
        }

        function changeBtts(style) {
            document.getElementById("Heading_BothToScore").style.display = style;
            //document.getElementById("Heading_BothToScoreYES").style.display = style;
            //document.getElementById("Heading_BothToScoreNO").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_Home_Last5_BTTS").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_Away_Last5_BTTS").style.display = style;
            changeStyle('btts', style);
        }

        function changeDoubleChance(style) {
            document.getElementById("Heading_DoubleChance").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_DoubleChanceHD").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_DoubleChanceDA").style.display = style;
            document.getElementById("Heading_DoubleChanceHA").style.display = style;
            changeStyle('doubleChance', style);
        }


        function changeOddsInGridview(odd) {
            /* 1= 1x2, 2= U/O 2.5, 3= BTTS, 4= Double chance*/
            //Hide over under
            changeOverUnder("none");
            //Hide over under
            changeBtts("none");
            //Hide double
            changeDoubleChance("none");
            //hide all
            changeWinLose("none");

            switch (odd) {
                case 1:
                    //show 1x2
                    changeWinLose("table-cell");
                    break;
                case 2:
                    //Show over under
                    changeOverUnder("table-cell");
                    break;
                case 3:
                    //Show btts
                    changeBtts("table-cell");
                    break;
                case 4:
                    //Show double
                    changeDoubleChance("table-cell");
                    break;
                default:
                    //show 1x2
                    changeWinLose("table-cell");


            }
        }
    }


</script>

<script>

    function changeFooter() {
        var windowHeight = $(window).height();
        var bodyHeight = $("#body").height();
        var footerHeight = $("#footer_subnav").height();
        var dif = windowHeight - bodyHeight - footerHeight - 40 - 77;
        var body = document.getElementById("body");
        body.setAttribute("style", "padding-bottom:" + dif + "px");
    }

    $(changeFooter());
    window.addEventListener("resize", changeFooter);

</script>
